Encore live probe
Base URL https://hackathon.api.qloo.com. GET only, X-Api-Key header, at most 3 calls at once, 20 s timeout, one retry on HTTP 429.
Only field names, JSON types, counts, statuses and our own labels are printed. No value from a Qloo answer is.
